_source line 1224 · 2026-05-15T16:12:07.023Z_

<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
Key findings from the logs:

**1.5B d005 s123 eval (GPU 0)**: On GSM8K, at item 1305/1319 at ~2:24 elapsed. Almost done with GSM8K. After that it still has ARC and HumanEval to do. The bitsandbytes error is non-fatal (just a warning).

**1.5B d025 s123 eval (GPU 1)**: On HumanEval, at item 80/164 at ~8:46 elapsed. It's further along than d005 eval — it already finished MMLU, GSM8K, ARC, and is halfway through HumanEval. 

The memory difference makes sense now — GPU 1 is running code generation on HumanEval which is more memory-intensive than the multiple choice tasks on GPU 0, so the 10455 MiB usage is expected and not indicative of a second process. I'm also seeing the 3B para_aligned model results with MMLU at 0.6461, GSM8K at 0.5000, and ARC at 0.8192.

Looking at the next set of results for the d025_s123_para_aligned variant, I notice the grep is only capturing three accuracy metrics (MMLU, GSM8K, and ARC) but not HumanEval — that's likely because HumanEval reports pass@1 rather than accuracy, or the JSON structure differs. I should also check on the status of the 3B aligned evals still running on GPUs 2 and 3, plus track what's happening with the CmdStan sweep process.

Now comparing the para_aligned results across seeds, I'm seeing something interesting: d005_s123_para_aligned has GSM8K=0.5000, which is dramatically higher than d005_s42_para_aligned at 0.1491 — that's a significant variance between seeds that's worth investigating.

The counterintuitive part is that d005 (5% contamination) is outperforming d025 on GSM8K in the para_aligned setting, which doesn't align with expectations since paraphrased questions should minimize memorization benefits. I'm wondering if the format confound from D044 is playing a role here, since d005 includes some GSM8K training data while d000 doesn't, but the paraphrasing should theoretically neutralize that advantage. not critical yet — I need the aligned results for d005 and d025 with seed 123 to properly compute the delta-of-deltas comparison.

Let me check on the GPU situation and what experiments are queued up. GPUs 4 and 5 just freed up after the para_aligned evals finished, and I've got three experiments waiting for resources: the 3B SFT variants and the signals experiment. The agent should be assigning these to the available GPUs now. I also notice there are some CPU-only processes running — the extreme gamma simulation and a CmdStan MCMC chain — so those aren't competing for GPU time.

I should prepare a status update for the Director covering the para_aligned completion, the freed GPU capacity, progress on the 1.5B evals, and that anomaly in the GSM8K results, but first I want to check how the 3B aligned evals are progressing.
```

</details>

<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
The eval_results.json for these s123 para_aligned runs only have `model_path` and `paraphrased` and `benchmarks` — no checkpoint_source, seed, dosage, or adapter info. That's a different format from the s42 runs I saw earlier (which had checkpoint_source). This could be because these were submitted by a different worker.

The directory names tell us: Qwen2.5-3B_d025_s123_para_aligned and Qwen2.5-3B_d005_s123_para_aligned. The SFTs these come from are exp_001_sft_3B_d005_s123 and exp_001_sft_3B_d025_s123, which completed successfully.


Now I'm looking at what's actually running — GPUs 4 and 5 are idle, and there are several experiments stuck in PREPARING status: the d025 and d000 v3 SFTs, the signals experiment, and two eval runs that show as PREPARING but have actually finished. The registry seems to be out of sync with the actual state.

I need to understand why the d000_v3 experiment was killed and check on the para_aligned eval results to compare cross-seed consistency between the s123 and s42 runs. Let me also see how far along the 3B aligned evals are on GPUs 2 and 3 before I report back to the Director about the idle GPUs and the registry sync issue.

Looking at the GPU state more carefully — the latest nvidia-smi shows GPUs 0-3 actively running evals (1.5B on 0-1, 3B aligned on 2-3), but GPU 4 had a process that's no longer in /proc, and GPU 5 is completely idle. Between the two queries, GPU 4 also freed up.

This is a critical utilization problem — I have two GPUs sitting idle while experiments are waiting to run. Let me check the d000_v3 situation and the current experiment queue to figure out what should be running next.
```

</details>

Now I have a clear picture:

**3B d005 s123 aligned eval (GPU 2)**: On GSM8K, 440/1319 (33%), ~58 min elapsed, ETA ~2h. Still has ARC + HumanEval after that. Total ETA ~5-6h more.

**3B d025 s123 aligned eval (GPU 3)**: On GSM8K, 591/1319 (45%), ~73 min elapsed, ETA ~1.5h for GSM8K. Still has ARC + HumanEval. Total ETA ~4-5h more.


The para_aligned results are in for both d005 and d025 variants. d025 shows stronger performance on MMLU and HumanEval, while d005 performs better on GSM8K. I can see d005_s42_para_aligned had an MMLU score of 0.6542, which sits between the two s123 variants.

Now looking at the full s42 para_aligned results, there's a striking discrepancy in the GSM8K scores. The d005_s42 variant scored only 0.1491 on GSM8K compared to 0.5000 for d005_s123—that's a massive 0.35 point drop. Meanwhile, d025 shows the opposite trend, with d025_v2_s42 at 0.5482 outperforming d025_s123's 0.4254. The d005_s42 GSM8K score looks like an outlier, and I'm noticing a pattern where the s42 results seem inconsistent across the different model variants.

This could point to seed sensitivity affecting how well the model learns chain-of-thought reasoning at low contamination levels, or there might be something specific about how the d005_s123 checkpoint was trained that gave it an advantage on GSM8K despite the lower dose.

The key takeaway is that individual seed results are too noisy to trust — I need to look at aggregated multi-seed patterns to draw reliable conclusions. HumanEval performance is predictably low for a 3B model, which tracks with what I'd expect.

Now I'm checking the GPU status to see where things stand with the current training runs. Looking at the GPU queue more carefully, GPU 0 is nearly done with GSM8K on the 1.5B d005 model and still has ARC and HumanEval ahead, which should take another 1-2 hours. GPU 1 is running the 1.5B d025 HumanEval eval and is about halfway through—should free up in roughly 10-15 minutes. With GPUs 4 and 5 already idle and GPU 1 coming available soon, I can start submitting the three pending experiments.

But I'm noticing something in the registry: the para_aligned evals for d005 and d025 with seed 123 are marked as PREPARING, yet the `.done` files show they've actually completed. The agent hasn't synced these status updates yet.

Looking closer, these registry entries lack PID and command fields entirely — they were registered as PREPARING but never went through the normal submit_training_job flow. The agent likely ran them manually in tmux without updating the registry to reflect RUNNING or completion status.

So I need to flag this to the Director: the para_aligned evals are done and GPUs 4/5 are now free, which means I can immediately submit the waiting PREPARING experiments. I'm also seeing a concerning variance in GSM8K performance across seeds for d005 — seed 42 shows 0.1491 while seed 123 shows 0.5000, which suggests there's significant instability I need to investigate.
```

_source line 1290 · 2026-05-15T16:13:29.350Z_

<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
Critical finding: Both s123 SFT checkpoints (d005_s123 and d025_s123) have **lora_alpha=32** already! This is good — it means the s123 experiments already used the correct alpha=32 value.

But from the DONE registry entries, I can see:
- `exp_001_sft_d100_3B_v2`: lora_alpha=16 (SUPERSEDED by D055)
- `exp_001_sft_3B_d000_v2`: lora_alpha=16 (SUPERSEDED by D055)


- The notes confirm that d000_v2 was superseded because it used alpha=16 while the original d000 already had alpha=32, and d100_v2 was similarly rerun with the correct alpha=32 value as D055.
- I'm now checking the remaining configurations: d025_v2 (alpha=16, needs rerun), s42 d005, and both s123 d000 and d100.
- Looking at the para_aligned evaluations, I see d000_s42 used the original checkpoint with alpha=32 (correct), but d100_s42 used the v2 checkpoint with alpha=16 (incorrect).
- The D055 spec calls for three specific reruns: d025_s42, d100_s42, and d100_s123 all need alpha=32, which matches what I'm seeing in the PREPARING entries, though I still need to verify what alpha value the s123 d100 checkpoint currently has.

Looking at the summary, d000_s123 and d005_s123 are already confirmed at alpha=32, and d025_s123 just checked out at alpha=32 too—so only d100_s123 actually needs the rerun. The core problems are that these three SFT jobs are stuck in PREPARING without launching, the para_aligned evals for d005_s123 and d025_s123 finished but the registry hasn't updated, and I've confirmed s123 is using alpha=32 across the board.

The d100_s42 para_aligned eval ran with the old alpha=16 checkpoint, so it'll need re-evaluation once the alpha=32 SFT finishes. Signals_3B_v2 is still waiting. The immediate priority is figuring out why GPUs 4 and 5 are idle when those D055 SFTs should be running—I should also check if that new process on GPU 0 (PID 588950) is one of them. I need to get a report to the Director about what I've found.
```

5. **exp_001_sft_3B_d005_s123** and **exp_001_sft_3B_d025_s123** (updated 15:08 and 14:48) — Both SFT runs complete
6. **exp_001_eval_3B_d000_s123_aligned** (updated 14:36) — Eval finished, and there's another eval run starting around 15:31 that got cut off
7. **exp_008_base_pretrained_eval_v2** (updated 14:21) — Base pretrained eval

Most of these are repeats from earlier reviews. The interesting comparison is between the d000 s123 aligned eval (mmlu=0.6332, gsm8k=0.1812, arc=0.8328, humaneval=0.0854) and the d000 s42 version (mmlu=0.6295, gsm8k=0.3525, arc=0.8183), where GSM8K drops significantly from 0.3525 to 0.1812 — that's a notable performance gap I should investigate.

Since both are d000 (no contamination), this variance is purely from seed differences in SFT plus the format confound, which reinforces the GSM8K instability pattern I've been seeing. The base pretrained model without any instruction tuning scores near random on MMLU and ARC (around 0.25 for 4-choice questions) and essentially zero on GSM8K and HumanEval, which is expected for an untuned model. I've sent my report and now I'm waiting for the agent to submit the PREPARING experiments to the freed GPUs, plus monitoring two eval runs—the 1.5B d025 s123 should finish in about 10-15 minutes on GPU 1, while the 1.5B d005 s123 will take longer on GPU 0. I should also review the decisions.yaml file to catch any new decisions made since I last checked at D055.

D050\\n- id: D052\\n  date: 2026-05-15 15:41 UTC\\n  summary: 'D052: IRT gamma 方向不稳定——不 pivot，等更多数据后重评估 Claim 1 措辞'\\n  rationale: Stratified IRT 发现 MMLU gamma 在 1.5B (+1.47) 和 3B (-5.70) 间翻转，pooled 4-bench\\n    IRT 完全未收敛 (rhat=1.74)。但仿真在 N=20+oracle 下 coverage 90%，确认方法本身 sound。根因是 N=4-8 不足\\n    + 信号噪声。Claim 3 (identifiability) 实际被强化。Claim 1 需从 \\\"gamma 方向是 benchmark 稳定属性\\\" 调整为\\n    \\\"benchmark×scale 交互作用，N<10 时无法稳定估计\\\"。不 panic pivot，等当前 eval batch 完成增加 N 后重跑 IRT\\n    再做最终判断。Pooled IRT 需重设计（加 d000 对照 + 均衡 dosage）。\\n  related_experiments:\\n  - exp_id: exp_006_stratified_irt_schemeB\\n    role: evidence\\n    result: MMLU gamma sign flip across scales\\n  - exp_id: exp_006_pooled_4bench_irt\\n    role: evidence\\n    result: rhat=1.74, ESS=6, 未收敛\\n  - exp_id: exp_004_extreme_gamma_sim\\n    role: evidence\\n    result: N=20 oracle CI95 coverage 90-100%\\n  depends_on:\\n  - D039\\n  - D035\\n  - D036\\n- id: D053\\n  date: 2026-05-15 15:49 UTC\\n  summary: 废弃联合IRT拟合和Stratified IRT结果，论文只引用per-benchmark独立IRT拟合\\n  rationale: 'Joint IRT: rhat=1.74, ESS=6.1, 完全未收敛，N_MODELS=6对4655 items过于复杂。Stratified\\n    IRT: N=4-6的stratum自由度不足，MMLU gamma在1.5B为+1.47、3B为-5.70符号翻转，无物理机制支持，是估计噪声。Per-benchmark独立拟合(N=10-18)更可靠，ARC\\n    gamma=+0.49 CI排除0、HumanEval gamma=-1.05 CI排除0，支持benchmark-dependent contamination\\n    effect核心叙事。'\\n  related_experiments:\\n  - exp_id: exp006_pooled_4bench_irt_v2\\n    role: evidence\\n    result: joint unconverged; per-benchmark ARC/HumanEval significant\\n  - exp_id: exp006_stratified_irt_scheme_b\\n    role: evidence\\n    result: gamma sign flips across scales, N too small\\n  depends_on: []\\n- id: D054\\n  date: 2026-05-15 15:49 UTC\\n  summary: Para_aligned d000需用v2 checkpoint重跑，消除checkpoint版本混杂\\n  rationale: Reviewer确认d000 para_aligned用原始checkpoint、d100用v2 checkpoint，delta-of-deltas被checkpoint版本差异混杂。aligned比较全用v2无此问题。需重跑d000\\n    para_aligned使用v2 checkpoint保持一致性。\\n  related_experiments:\\n  - exp_id: exp_001_eval_3B_d000_s42_para_aligned\\n    role: confounded\\n    result: uses original checkpoint instead of v2\\n  - exp_id: exp_001_eval_3B_d100_s42_para_aligned\\n    role: reference\\n    result: uses v2 checkpoint\\n  depends_on: []\\n- id: D055\\n  date: 2026-05-15 15:52 UTC\\n  summary: 修正 D050/D051：基于实际 alpha 验证，3B 仅需重跑 3 个 SFT（d025_s42, d100_s42, d100_s123），弃用\\n    d000_v2\\n  rationale: Agent 实际检查所有 3B checkpoint 的 lora_alpha，发现混合分布而非之前假设的\\\"全部 alpha=16\\\"。d000/d005\\n    两个 seed 已是 alpha=32，仅 d025_s42/d100_s42/d100_s123 是 alpha=16。d000_v2（两个 seed）虽是\\n    alpha=16 但有原始 d000（alpha=32）可替代，弃用 v2。修复 train_sft.py argparse 参数解析防止默认值覆盖 CLI\\n    参数。总重跑量从 D050 的 5 个降至 3 个，节省约 40% GPU 时间。\\n  related_experiments:\\n  -     "type": "skill_listing",
    "content": "- update-config: Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Automated behaviors (\"from now on when X\", \"each time X\", \"whenever X\", \"before/after X\") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- simplify: Review changed code for reuse, quality, and efficiency, then fix any issues found.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- schedule: Create, update, list, or run scheduled remote agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule. - When the user wants to schedule a recurring remote agent, set up automated tasks, create a cron job for Claude Code, or manage their scheduled agents/routines. Also use when the user wants a one-time scheduled run (\"run this once at 3pm\", \"remind me to check X tomorrow\").\n- claude-api: Build, debug, and optimize Claude API / Anthropic SDK apps. Apps built with this skill should include prompt caching. Also handles migrating existing Claude API code between Claude model versions (4.5 → 4.6, 4.6 → 4.7, retired-model replacements).\nTRIGGER when: code imports `anthropic`/`@anthropic-ai/sdk`; user asks for the Claude API, Anthropic SDK, or Managed Agents; user adds/modifies/tunes a Claude feature (caching, thinking, compaction, tool use, batch, files, citations, memory) or model (Opus/Sonnet/Haiku) in a file; questions about prompt caching / cache hit rate in an Anthropic SDK project.\nSKIP: file imports `openai`/other-provider SDK, filename like `*-openai.py`/`*-generic.py`, provider-neutral code, general programming/ML.\n- init: Initialize a new CLAUDE.md file with codebase documentation\n- review: Review a pull request\n- security-review: Complete a security review of the pending changes on the current branch",
